where for
v\in\mathbb{R}^{q}
and
\diag(v)\in\mathbb{R}^{q\times q}
,
\bm{L}_{q}
is defined
so that

\vect(\diag(v))=\bm{L}_{q}\,v
.
Similarly,

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\bm{D}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon}}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;-\diag\!\big(\mu_{b,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{-2}\big)\,\diag\!\left(\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{a,F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\,2\,\diag\!\big(\mu_{a,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\oslash\mu_{b,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{3}\big)\,\diag\!\left(\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{b,F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{D}^{(2)}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;-\bigl(\bm{I}\otimes\diag(\mu_{b,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{-2})\bigr)\,\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\vect\left(\diag(\mu_{a,F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})))\right)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle\qquad+\,2\bigl(\bm{I}\otimes\diag(\mu_{a,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\oslash\mu_{b,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{3})\bigr)\,

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\vect\left(\diag(\mu_{b,F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})))\right)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;-\bm{B}^{D}_{2}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{a,F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{B}^{D}_{3}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{b,F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

where
\bm{B}^{D}_{2}:=\bigl(\bm{I}\otimes\diag(\mu_{b,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{-2})\bigr)\,\bm{L}_{d+d(d+1)/2}\,
and
\bm{B}^{D}_{3}:=\,2\bigl(\bm{I}\otimes\diag(\mu_{a,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\oslash\mu_{b,F}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{3})\bigr)\,\bm{L}_{d+d(d+1)/2}\,
.
By the chain rule,

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}\bigl(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})\bigr)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

with
\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}:=\left.\frac{\partial\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta})}{\partial\bm{\eta}}\right|_{\bm{\eta}=\bm{\eta}_{0}}
.
So

\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{D}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=\bm{B}^{D}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{B}^{D}\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

where

\bm{B}^{D}=\begin{pmatrix}0&-\bm{B}_{1}^{D}\\
-\bm{B}_{2}^{D}&\bm{B}_{3}^{D}\end{pmatrix}.

Recall that

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=\Cov_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl(Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\bigr)

\displaystyle=\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\Big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad-\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}.

Under the FDCM or the FICM, differentiating with respect
to
\varepsilon
at
\varepsilon=0
yields

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\,\vect(\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\vect\Big(\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})^{T}\bigr]\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle+\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\,\vect\Big(\E_{H_{0}}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\,Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}\big]\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle-\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\,\vect\Big(\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})^{T}\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle-\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\,\vect\Big(\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

Using
\vect(uv^{T})=v\otimes u
, the last two terms become

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\,\vect(\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\vect\Big(\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})^{T}\bigr]\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle+\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\,\vect\Big(\E_{H_{0}}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\,Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}\big]\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle-\left(\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes\bm{I}+\bm{I}\otimes\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right)\left.\frac{\partial\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

Since the expectation is taken with respect to the fixed distribution
H_{0}
, the only

\varepsilon
–dependence is through
\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})
. Hence, by the product rule,

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\Big(Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}\Big)\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})^{T}

\displaystyle\quad+Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

Moreover, by the chain rule,

\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta})}{\partial\bm{\eta}}\right|_{\bm{\eta}=\bm{\eta}_{0}}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\,

\displaystyle\vect\Big(\E_{H_{0}}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))\,Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}\big]\Big)\Big|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\;\E_{H_{0}}\!\Bigg[\vect\Big(\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})^{T}+Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon}))^{T}}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\;\E_{H_{0}}\!\Bigg[\big(Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes\bm{I}+\bm{I}\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\big)\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta})}{\partial\bm{\eta}}\right|_{\bm{\eta}=\bm{\eta}_{0}}\Bigg]\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\;\bm{B}^{Z}_{1}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

with
\bm{B}^{Z}_{1}:=\E_{H_{0}}\!\Bigg[\big(Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes\bm{I}+\bm{I}\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\big)\left.\frac{\partial Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta})}{\partial\bm{\eta}}\right|_{\bm{\eta}=\bm{\eta}_{0}}\Bigg]
.
Thus

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\,\vect(\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle+\left(\bm{B}^{Z}_{1}-\bm{B}^{Z}_{2}\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}\right)\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}-\bm{B}^{Z}_{2}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

with
\bm{B}^{Z}_{2}:=\left(\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes\bm{I}+\bm{I}\otimes\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right)
.

Then,

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\sigma_{\theta}(H_{\varepsilon})^{2}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{M}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+2\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\bm{M}_{0}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{J}_{\varepsilon}}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\Big\{\bm{A}_{K}\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{K}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{A}_{D}\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{D}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\bm{A}_{\Sigma}\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}\Big\}+2\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\bm{M}_{0}\bm{J}_{2,0}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\theta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

(A.137)

where we have set

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{K}

\displaystyle:=(\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,0}\bm{D}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{I}_{d+d(d+1)/2})+(\bm{I}\otimes\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,0}\bm{D}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{P}_{K},

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{D}

\displaystyle:=(\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,0}\otimes\bm{K}_{0})+(\bm{K}_{0}\otimes\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,0})\,\bm{P}_{D},

\displaystyle\bm{A}_{\Sigma}

\displaystyle:=(\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}\otimes\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}).

Moreover,

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{K}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=-(\bm{K}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{K}_{0})\bm{H}_{\theta\theta}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\theta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}-(\bm{K}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{K}_{0})\bm{H}_{\theta\eta}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

(A.138)

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\vect(\bm{D}_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\bm{B}^{D}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{B}^{D}\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

(A.139)

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial\,\vect(\bm{\Sigma}_{ab,\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\left(\bm{B}^{Z}_{1}-\bm{B}^{Z}_{2}\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}\right)\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle\qquad-\bm{B}^{Z}_{2}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

(A.140)

Substituting (
A.138
)–(
A.140
) into (
A.137
) and
collecting terms yields

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\sigma_{\theta}(H_{\varepsilon})^{2}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\bm{m}_{\theta}^{T}\bm{K}_{0}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}P_{F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{m}_{\eta}^{T}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle+\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}+\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right|_{\varepsilon=0},

(A.141)

where

\displaystyle\bm{m}_{\theta}^{T}

\displaystyle:=-(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{K}\,(\bm{K}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{K}_{0})\,\bm{H}_{\theta\theta}+2\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\bm{M}_{0}\bm{J}_{2,0},

\displaystyle\bm{m}_{\eta}^{T}

\displaystyle:=-(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{K}\,(\bm{K}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{K}_{0})\,\bm{H}_{\theta\eta}+(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{D}\,\bm{B}^{D}\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}

\displaystyle\qquad+(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{\Sigma}\,(\bm{B}^{Z}_{1}-\bm{B}^{Z}_{2}\bm{D}_{\mu,\eta}),

\displaystyle\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T}

\displaystyle:=(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{D}\,\bm{B}^{D}-(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{\Sigma}\,\bm{B}^{Z}_{2},

\displaystyle\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}

\displaystyle:=(\bm{J}_{0}^{T}\otimes\bm{J}_{0}^{T})\,\bm{A}_{\Sigma}\,.

We also used

\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\theta}(H_{\varepsilon})}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=\bm{K}_{0}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}P_{F}(H_{\varepsilon},\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}.

Let

g(X):=Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0}).

By (
A.134
),

\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}[g(X)]=\sum_{j=0}^{d}\delta_{j}(\varepsilon)\sum_{I\in\ell_{j}}\E_{H(I,\bm{c})}[g(X)].

Under the FDCM we have
H_{\varepsilon}=H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c})
, and

\delta_{0}(\varepsilon)=(1-\varepsilon),\;\delta_{1}(\varepsilon)=\cdots=\delta_{d-1}(\varepsilon)=0
, and

\delta_{d}(\varepsilon)=\varepsilon
.
Thus,

\E_{H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c})}[g(X)]=(1-\varepsilon)\E_{H_{0}}[g(X)]+\varepsilon\,g(\bm{c}).

and

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\E_{H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c})}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})-\E_{H_{0}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr].

Moreover from (
A.136
), we have

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}P_{F}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c}),\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=\bm{D}_{0}Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0}).

and moreover from (
A.135
)

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c}),\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})-\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0}).

Thus, from (
A.141
), we have

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\sigma_{\theta}^{2}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;\bm{m}_{\eta}^{T}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c}))}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}-\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T}\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})

(A.142)

\displaystyle\quad-\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}\,\E_{H_{0}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]

\displaystyle\quad+(\bm{m}_{\theta}^{T}\bm{K}_{0}\,\bm{D}_{0}\,+\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T})\,Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})+\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}\!\bigl(Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr).

(A.143)

Thus

\displaystyle\IFu_{\mbox{\scriptsize{case}}}(\bm{c},\sigma_{\theta},H_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\,\sigma_{\theta,0}}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\sigma_{\theta}^{2}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{D},\bm{c}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\;m_{s,1}+\bm{m}_{s,2}^{T}\,\IFu_{\mbox{\scriptsize{case}}}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta},H_{0})+\bm{m}_{s,3}^{T}Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})+\bm{m}_{s,4}^{T}\!\bigl(Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr).

where

m_{s,1}:=-\frac{1}{2\sigma_{\theta,0}}\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T}\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})-\frac{1}{2\sigma_{\theta,0}}\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}\,\E_{H_{0}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr],

\bm{m}_{s,2}^{T}:=\frac{1}{2\sigma_{\theta,0}}\bm{m}_{\eta}^{T}\qquad\bm{m}_{s,3}^{T}:=\frac{1}{2\sigma_{\theta,0}}\Big(\bm{m}_{\theta}^{T}\bm{K}_{0}\,\bm{D}_{0}+\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T}\Big),\qquad\bm{m}_{s,4}^{T}:=\frac{1}{2\sigma_{\theta,0}}\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}.

Under the FICM we have

\delta_{0}(\varepsilon)=(1-\varepsilon)^{d},\qquad\delta_{1}(\varepsilon)=d(1-\varepsilon)^{d-1}\varepsilon,

so that
\delta_{0}(0)=1
,
\delta^{\prime}_{0}(0)=-d
,
\delta_{1}(0)=0
,
\delta_{1}^{\prime}(0)=d
, and

\delta_{j}(0)=\delta_{j}^{\prime}(0)=0
for all
j\geq 2
.
Thus

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\E_{H_{\varepsilon}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;-d\,\E_{H_{0}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]

\displaystyle\quad+d\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr],

where
H(j,\bm{c}):=H(\{j\},\bm{c})
.
Using this together with

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}P_{F}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{I},\bm{c}),\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=\bm{D}_{0}d\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})]

and

\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\mu_{Z_{F}}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{I},\bm{c}),\bm{\eta}_{0})\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=-d\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})+d\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\big],

we obtain from (
A.141
) the expanded FICM expression

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\sigma_{\theta}^{2}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{I},\bm{c}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}=

\displaystyle\;\bm{m}_{\theta}^{T}\bm{K}_{0}\bm{D}_{0}d\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\big]+\bm{m}_{\eta}^{T}\left.\frac{\partial\bm{\eta}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{I},\bm{c}))}{\partial\varepsilon}\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{m}_{\mu}^{T}\left(-d\,\mu_{Z_{F}}(H_{0},\bm{\eta}_{0})+d\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\big]\right)

\displaystyle\quad-d\,\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}\E_{H_{0}}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]

\displaystyle\quad+d\,\bm{m}_{\mathrm{dir}}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right).

Thus

\displaystyle\IFu_{\mbox{\scriptsize{cell}}}(\bm{c},\sigma_{\theta},H_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\,\sigma_{\theta,0}}\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial\varepsilon}\sigma_{\theta}^{2}(H(G_{\varepsilon}^{I},\bm{c}))\right|_{\varepsilon=0}

\displaystyle=\;dm_{s,1}+\bm{m}_{s,2}^{T}\,\IFu_{\mbox{\scriptsize{cell}}}(\bm{c},\bm{\eta},H_{0})+d\bm{m}_{s,3}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\big[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\big]\right)

\displaystyle+d\bm{m}_{s,4}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\E_{H(j,\bm{c})}\!\bigl[Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\otimes Z_{F}(X,\bm{\eta}_{0})\bigr]\right).

Combining these results with (
A.128
),
(
A.129
), (
A.130
),
and (
A.131
) completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

Appendix F
Additional simulation results

Figure
5
in the
main text shows the average
MSE
attained by
RIDGE, SEST, PENSE, CRM, REGCELL, SHOOT, and
cellMR in the presence of cellwise outliers,
casewise outliers, or both, without missing data.
When we also set 10% of randomly selected cells
to NA we obtain
Figure
11
.
Surprisingly, the curves of cellMR are almost the
same as in Figure
5
.
Of the other methods, only RIDGE is able to handle
missing values. Its curves are similar to before
in the setting
p=q=10
, but worsen in the
higher dimensions.
